#{#zom5a}\n\t\tif (e.key === 'Escape') {\n\t\t\tclose();\n\t\t\treturn;\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Arrow keys for panning when zoomed - #{#zom2b}\n\t\tif (zoom > MIN_ZOOM) {\n\t\t\tswitch (e.key) {\n\t\t\t\tcase 'ArrowUp':\n\t\t\t\t\tpanY += PAN_STEP;\n\t\t\t\t\te.preventDefault();\n\t\t\t\t\tbreak;\n\t\t\t\tcase 'ArrowDown':\n\t\t\t\t\tpanY -= PAN_STEP;\n\t\t\t\t\te.preventDefault();\n\t\t\t\t\tbreak;\n\t\t\t\tcase 'ArrowLeft':\n\t\t\t\t\tpanX += PAN_STEP;\n\t\t\t\t\te.preventDefault();\n\t\t\t\t\tbreak;\n\t\t\t\tcase 'ArrowRight':\n\t\t\t\t\tpanX -= PAN_STEP;\n\t\t\t\t\te.preventDefault();\n\t\t\t\t\tbreak;\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Optional keyboard shortcuts for zoom - touch2.clientX;\n\t\tconst dy = touch1.clientY - touch2.clientY;\n\t\treturn Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * #{#zom3a} Handle touch start for pinch zoom and pan\n\t */\n\tfunction handleTouchStart(e: TouchEvent) {\n\t\tif (e.touches.length === 2) {\n\t\t\t// Pinch zoom start\n\t\t\ttouchStartDistance = getTouchDistance(e);\n\t\t\ttouchStartZoom = zoom;\n\t\t\te.preventDefault();\n\t\t} else if (e.touches.length === 1) {\n\t\t\t// Check for double tap -
